S . C . 466 . - --In pursuence of clause ( 3 ) of article 77 of the 
Constitution of India , the President hereby makes the follow 
ing rules further to amend the Delegation of Financial Powers 
Rules, 1978, namely : -- 


1. (1 ) These rules may be called the Delegation of Finan 
cial Powers ( Amendment) Rules, 1993 . 

( 2) They shall come into force on the date of their Pub . 
lication in the Official Gazetto . 


2. In Schedule VI to the Delegation of Financial Powers 
Rules , 1978 , in the Note bolow the Table . for the words and 
figures " Rs. 2 . 50 ", the words and figures " Rs. 3. 50 " shall bo 
substituted.

प्रसः, केन्द्रीय सरकार , सक्त अधिनियम की धारा 5 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग मरते हुए 
इस अधिसूचना से उपाबद्ध अनुसूची . में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार अजित करने की घोषणा करती है ;

( 4 ) द्वारा प्रवत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह निदेश 
देती है कि उक्त भूमियों के उपयोग का ग्राधिकार केन्द्रीय 
सरकार में मिहित होने के बजाए विधि विल्लगमों ने मनम 
होकर , इंडियन आयल कॉर्पोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) 
प्रधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) (जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के 
पैट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं . 
का . मा . 546 तारीख 15 फरवरी, 1992 द्वारा पेट्रोलियम 
के परिवहन के लिए पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनार्थ उक्स 
मधिसूचना में उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिष्टि भूमि में उपयोग 
के अधिकारों के पर्जन के अपने प्राशय की घोषणा की थी ; 

राजपत्र अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 
09 मार्च। 1992 को उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी । 

उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के 
अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को रिपोर्ट 
देवी है ;

का . मा . 475 : केन्द्रीय सरकार ने , पेट्रोलियम और खनिज 
पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का मर्जन ) 
मधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) (जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात् उक्त मधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 3 की उप 
धारा ( 1 ) के अधीन जारी की गई भारत सरकार के पैट्रो 
लियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं . फा . मा . 
624 तारीख 22 फरवरी, 1992 , द्वारा पेट्रोलियम के परि 
बहन के लिए पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजनार्थ उक्त प्रधि 
सूचना से उपवास अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के 
अधिकारों के अर्जन के अपने माशय की घोषणा की थी । 


राजपत्र अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को सारीख 17 
मार्च 1992 की उपलब्ध करा दी गई थी ; 

उस अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के 
अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी केन्द्रीय सरकार को रिपोर्ट

New Delhi, the 16th February , 1993 
S . O . 478 , — Whercas by the notification of the Governdunt 
of India in the Ministry of Petroleum and Natural Gas No . 
S . O . 628, dated the 22nd February , 1992 , issued under sub 
noction ( 1 ) of section 3 of tho Petroleum and Minerals Pipo 
lines (Acquisition of Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 
1962 ) hereinafter referred to as the sald Act), tho Central 
Government declared its intention to acquire the right of user 
in the lands specified in the Schedule appended to that noti 
fication for the purpose of laying pipolia for the transport 
of petroleum ; 

And whereas the copies of the said Gazette notification 
were made availablo to the public on 17th March , 1992 ;

section ( 2 ) of section 11 of the Indian Medical Council Act, 
1956 ( 102 of 1956 ), the Central Government aftor conşulting 
the Medical Council of India hereby makes the following 
further amendments in the First Schedule to the said Aci,

अधिनियम की प्रथम अनुसूचो में निम्नलिखित और 
संशोधन करती है , अर्थात् : 
( 1 ) प्रथम अन सूची में , "मद्रास विश्वविद्यालय "

निम्नलिखित प्रविष्टि अन्तः स्थापित की जाएगी 
अर्थात् : - - 
"डिप्लोमा इन मेडिसिन डी . एम . ( ज . वि . ) " 

( जठरान विज्ञान )

विद्यालय मद्रास " शीर्षक के नीचे की प्रवि 
ष्टियों के पश्चात निम्नलिखित प्रविष्टि 
अत: स्थापित की जाएगी , अर्थात्: --

According to the bank management, the relief claimed 
by the workmen is barred by the principle of ostoppel/ 
acquiescere as the civil suit filed by the workmken was un 
conditionally withdrawn by them . Ex. 22 is the copy of 
the Judgment in Regular Civil Suit No. 1 of 1984 filed by 
tho workmen in question against the State Bank of Saurashtra , 
It is soon therefrom that the workmon plaintiffs had fled 
that suit to get an injunction rostralning the defendant bank 
from terminating the services of those workmen plaintiffs . 
However that suit was disposed of by the Civil Judge ( J . D .), 
Porbundar hy tho Judgment dated 31 - 1 - 1986 on tho ground 
that the competent authority in the matter was he labour 
court under the Industrial Disputo Act, and not the Civil 
Court, As such that suit was not withdrawn by the work 
men , but it was disposed of by the Civil Court for the said 
reason . As such the alleycd relief claimed by thc workman 
under the present reference is not barred by the principio 
estoppel /acquiescon .

As regards the issuo No . 5 , the Maharana Mills -has already

regarding the cclosure of the mills what services of all 
the workmen including the workkmen in question wero termi 
nated by tho Maharana Mily. As such I find that the 
present referonce is quite vaid and legal. 

ISSUE No. 5 is therefore found accordingly .

has argun. 1 thut on daily wago the petitioner was mechanic

unjustifier and against tho natural justice and the manage 
ment be directed to give the scale of Rs. 210 -290 to the 
petitioner. There is no force in this contention. There is 
no evidenco on the record that the Acrylces of tho petitioner 
from daily wages to work charge cadre was converted , 
Rather the pettioner was given fresh appointment in work 
charge cadre . Ex W2 is the offer of appointment dated 
11 -8 - 1979 in which the petitioner was offered the post of 
store helper in the work charge cadre in the scale of 
Rs. 196 - 232 . The petitioner had accepted this offer. Upon 
his acceptance the petitioner was appointed in the said scale of 
Rs. 196 -232 as ktore keeper . It was up to the petitioner to 
accept or not to accept th : offer. Since he had given accent 
ance to th3 said offer he is to be governed with the terms 
and con .litions laid down in the offer of appointment. He 
can not raise any dispute regarding terms and conditions 
after so many years . Admitted position is that offer of 
protection of designation was made in the year 1980 al 
though the petitioner was appointed afreshw. c, f. 21- 8 - 1979 
and arain cannot claim benefit of tho said circular . The 
petitioner hos alun don not placed on the record the omist 
circular for the judicial scrutiny .

1. The Central Government Ministry of Labour New 
Delhi, vide its notification no . L - 41012 / 98 / 89 -1. R . DU dated

to this Tribunal :

Ullah w . e. f. 4 - 10 -81 ? If not, what relief tho work 
man concernod is entitled to ? 


2 . The case of the Union is that the workman was appointed 
48 a fittor Khalasi in tho Locoshed Northem Rly on 5 - 3 -80 . 
However , his services wero terminated w . e . f. 4 . 9 -81 without 
compliance of the provisions of section 25F I. D . Act. Tho 
Union , bas , thereforo, prayed for the reinstatement of the 
workman with full back wages and all consoquential benefits . 

The case is contested by tho management. The management 
pload that the alleged workman had nover worked as alleged 
by the Union . Vido P . S . No. 6167 and 6963, tho Rly Board 
has imposed a ban on the engagemont of casual labour / 
substitutes except under the specific orders of the D . R . M . 
Later on vido P . S . no. 7716 a casual labour / substituto could 
be engaged only with the prior approval of the General 
Manager. The management further plead that it had como 
to tho notice of the railway administration that a number of 
persons had been fraudulently shown on the mustor roll with 
the copnivance of some members of the office staff without 
any sanction from the competent uuthority . As a result of it 
tho railway administration suffered a loss of crores of rupees 
by way of wages . Tho matter was taken up by the vigilence

nated all the fictitious persons who were shown on the mustor 
toll . Some 2000 such persons were shown on the mustor roll 
against casuality of total strength of 1500 cmployees . Since 
overy thing was illogal and fraudulent, the Union is entitled 
to no relief. In fact there is no valid industrial dispute within 
the meaning of section 2 (k ) of the I. D . Act. It is also pleaded 
by the management that the alleged Union is neither recognised 
by the management nor it is registered under the Trade Union

6 . I have heard both the parties and gono through the 
written arguments Aled by the parties . 

7. Rep . appearing on behalf of the workman has argued 
that articlo of charges have not been proved by the respdt. 
management against the petitioner and thus dismissal of the 
petitioner is not tenable in tho eyes of law and violativa of 
the principle of natural justice . On the contrary counsel 
appearing on behalf of tho respdt, management has arguod 
that the petitioner is a habitual troublo creator for which 
ho has been chargeshcoted and punished on many occasions . 
I am not convinced with the arguments advanced by the 
counsel for the respdt. management. The petitioner was 
charge sheeted for two charges . First chargo relatos to tho 
incident dated 25 - 11 - 1983 on which dato ho ontered tha 
dopot promises under the drunken conditions and misbehaved 
with the colleagues and members of the F . S . D . Tad Chargo 
le that he is habitual trouble creator and did not improvd 
his behaviour even after his transfer to another station . 
So far first charge is concerned there is absolutely do cvi 
dence on the record to connect tho petii ner with the inci 
dent dated 25- 11- 1983, Giat Singh Asst. Manager (Depot) 
to whom it is stated that the peti: inner had misbehaved and 
abusod noíthor cited as a witricss nor prú luced as prosecu 
tion witnons . Other wito 409 produced during thc course 
of enquiry has also not supported the case of the respdt. 
management as they have failed to connect the petitioner 
with the incident dated 25. 1 : - 1983 . Not oply this the 
potitioner has also been cx toated of the wall charge by 
the Managing Director of the Respdt. manu- piert in its 
order passed on 7 -11-1988 which is Ex W34 in the review 
application of tho petitioner in thich it hay tren said that 
thore is no direct evidenco of drunken misbehaviour and the 
two witnetses who bas test!fy that this officinl is smelling 
of liquor is not enough to sustain the charge . 

Further with regard to the 2rd charge thut the petitioner 
is habitual troublo creator, there is no specific instances in 
the charge. It is settled law that thc charte has to be 
pocific relating to tho date and timo of the incident. Tho 
chargcs cannot be in general. Further it is not denying 
the fact that on tarller cha: 0 sf misconduct the petitioner 
has been adequately punished in the name has been reflected 
in the orders passed by the Managing Director in the review 
application which 18 Ex , W34. Punishment on cirller charges 
also shall amounts to double punishment. Therefore extreme 
punishment of distrissal on the vague chargo that the poti 
tioner is habitual trouble creator can not stand . 

The rep . of the petitioner has also restricted his claim for 
foregoing back wagos altogether and has admitted to accept 
tbo punishment of two increments with cumulative effect . 

In view of the discussion made in the earlier paras tho 
Dctitioner has already been exenorated by the Managing 
Director in the review petition of the main charge and the 
extreme penalty of dismissal imposed on the subsequent 
charge on account of periodic misconduct is certainly excos 
sivo. Thus it is ordered that the positioner is reinstatod with
